The panel stays pinned when its controls are inapplicable. Controls show source-derived availability and reasons. Revealing it is H01 and cannot raise a logical window. A Y-action that needs a source Yard owner uses the deterministic preparation and route specified by the catalog; an audited complete logic entry may avoid a legacy object event.

## Programmable buttons and bookmarks: reference detail, not new DOS logic

Win16 map objects `2210–2217` and yard `230B–2312` are **eight programmable buttons each**, not fixed semantic slots. The original SYM places arrays `_mapUserButton` at DGROUP:0012 and `_yardUserButton` at DGROUP:0022. Reading the matching original NE data segment gives defaults **15,0,1,3,7,6,5,10** for both: Go to player, Behavior, Caste, Info, Music, cycle Speed, Pause, Silly. This was a binary read, not a deduction from icon appearance. Profile settings can change them.

The sixteen action values are: 0 Behavior; 1 Caste; 2 History; 3 Info; 4 showTrails; 5 Pause; 6 cycle speed; 7 Music; 8 Mystery; 9 Sound Effects; 10 Silly; 11 AutoTrack; 12 Events; 13 Messages; 14 Save; 15 show patch and go to player. `DoUserButtonUpdate` derives check states from GamePaused and OptionStates for toggles. Ctrl-drag customization and Shift-only-one-bar assignment are old interaction choices, not modern requirements.

Native recommendation: shared labelled command buttons with an optional Customize panel stored in host preferences. DOS equivalents are `FD13/14/15/16`, `FD41`, `FD43–46`, `FD32/33/36/31/34/35`, `FD05`, `000B`, and `0112` for Mystery. **showTrails has no proven identical standalone DOS command in this investigation**; do not transplant that Win16 variable or call its code. Its optional affordance needs a proven DOS mapping first.

Map bookmarks are dispatched by `2218–221E`; the inspected yard dispatcher handles `2313–2318`, while `ClearBookmarks` initializes seven paired slots including `2319`. That last-slot discrepancy is explicit reference debt, not license to manufacture a seventh live action. For a first native release, defer programmable bookmarks; optional host camera bookmarks can later store presentation positions and issue only existing, verified source navigation commands. They must not introduce Win16 save-state fields into DOS saves.

## How the Macintosh groups the same intent

The pinned resource fork hash is `3e73ef63500bed0c666db56a25b5a5951c09d9a266c8c682f373b2825050f351` from ISO hash `8e7518796dbf32db9ff483dcc49069d4d8ec6e4625918fe4d47b03de8cc5fb0b`. It has ten WIND, six MENU, sixteen DLOG/DITL pairs, two CNTL and **no ALRT resource type**. WIND 1000–1005 name Edit, Map, Behavior, Caste, History and Info; WIND 1006 is Message. It does not contain Win16's 0x2200/0x2300 ribbon definitions, nor a native object-ID correspondence to DOS.

MENU 302 exposes Edit/Map/Behavior/Caste/History/Info/Status. MENU 303 exposes Yard/House/Strategic/Graphic/Surface/Black Nest/Red Nest. MENU 304 exposes Auto Track/Music/Sound Effects/Events/Messages/Silly; MENU 305 Pause and four speeds. Mac File includes New/Open/Close/Save/Save As/Quit, and About belongs to Apple. This corroborates the functional grouping without requiring the old layout. CNTL 1000 and 1001 are hScroll/vScroll resources; tutorial STR# 11016 describes Control-drag scrolling. STR# 11086 describes help popups, and STR# 11102 describes click-hold recruitment. Exact Mac hidden tracking behavior, resource-to-DOS control mapping and per-view extra controls beyond these facts have not been proved; the missing scrolling report must not be silently replaced with inference.

# Settled owner decisions

The owner accepted the recommendations on 2026-11-15, with inspectors always live:

| Policy | Settled choice |
|---|---|
| Primary pan | Real canonical navigation N03; extra views are read-only H04. |
| Navigation bindings | Wheel zoom and middle-drag pan by default; wheel-pan/Ctrl+wheel-zoom preset. No world scrollbars. |
| Allocation | Labelled controls exposing exactly source-reachable values; ternary visualization may follow. |
| Inspectors | Always live. No Freeze, pinned-time copy or restore. One primary plus up to three inspectors. |
| Focus loss | Continue simulation, source clock and audio; no automatic Pause. |
| Saves | Manual source saves through native dialogs; no autosave. Backups deferred. |
| Art and text | Decode original 16-colour art as native assets; native system text; English first. |
| Controller | Out of scope. |

These are product decisions. Engineering proof gaps in E/T/U actions stay explicit.

Use a native Settings panel for host display/input/text/layout/audio-gain preferences. Music/effects enable and simulation speed remain S-actions. Master gain is postmix; separate music/effects gain needs tagged native streams without altering source command order. Controller support remains out of scope for this completion. Standard modern shortcuts and accessible native focus are the default; historical aliases are compatibility choices only where they do not conflict with text/workspace editing.

Classic retains its original VGA desktop/windows, bitmap fonts, FileSelect, scaling/filtering, input and rendering behavior. Host workspace layouts/settings are separate from classic and from game saves.

# Verification: action equivalence and passive invariance

**Action equivalence:** from the same canonical initial state and source seam, the same catalog action, parameters, modifiers and ordered samples must produce the same complete canonical state and source outputs, regardless of which native panel/control invoked it or how the workspace is arranged. Reference execution uses the documented complete canonical entry or original transaction for that action, including deterministic owner preparation. An Open Panel action is H01, not an undocumented FDxx operation. A final allocation value or pointer endpoint alone does not define an equivalent gesture when intermediate samples matter.

**Passive invariance:** without gameplay actions, canonical state must remain identical with panels visible/hidden/collapsed/tabbed/floating, with extra live read-only views, repaint rates, native focus, text search/hover, UI zoom, layout save/restore, DPI or monitor changes. Source logical-open/front state and source draw effects must be identical too. Do not mask them because they originated in UI code. A source cancel/release caused by an interrupted gameplay gesture is an explicit action outcome, not a passive exception. Focus loss continues execution; automatic focus-pause is excluded.

Verify at corresponding source seams using established state-view/acceptance machinery: relevant full state fingerprints, SaveRec projection, RNG streams, source tick/read/iteration markers, source event queues and accepted action ledger, menu/object selection state, logical-open/front/geometry, card/preset/graph state, transient messages/deadlines/animation selectors, source sound commands and wait results. Native layout/textures/immutable asset copies are host data excluded by ownership, not by value masks. Do not advance source time just to sample it. Existing bounded comparisons do not establish full state equivalence.

Required differential matrix:

| Case | Required result / negative control |
|---|---|
| Same action from Ant Controls, menu, keyboard and detached panel | Same semantic/action ledger and canonical state; no HWND/focus-dependent preparation or double delivery. |
| World click to same tile at different canvas sizes/zooms/layouts | Same source navigation prelude and tile/modifiers; source state/RNG match. Deliberately route using native client width to demonstrate a failing control. |
| Primary pan, plane changes, follow/go-to, overview/minimap | Exact source call/sample order, clamp and XOR-step behavior; no extra repaint-driven scroll calls. |
| Allocation presets/Auto/Manual/gesture | Fixed-point reachable outputs, preset save/load and release-only IdealCaste tail match; approximate independent slider normalization must fail. |
| Editor holds, scent/insecticide repetitions and queen placement | Same repeat samples and source RNG/counters, owner lease and cancel/commit. Coalescing to final tile must fail where repetitions matter. |
| Hide/close/tab source-related panels and Reset Layout during play | No win_Open/Close/ToTop calls; no draw suppression; canonical state equals stationary-layout control. |
| Add three inspectors; show another plane; repaint/close them | No SetMapPlane/global writes/source resource loads or RNG calls; occupied entities remain visible through music/cache changes. |
| Native first activation click and source queue flush | One accepted action after necessary preparation; held up/down transaction retained. Old raise-and-eat and delivery-before-flush are negative controls. |
| Source modal prompts, menus, help and files under every layout | Same wait/timeout/result/default/input cleanup/audio/simulation behavior. Restoring a layout cannot hide an unanswered task or accept it. |
| Music-enabled rebirth | The original retained trace shows life maps occupied with null source sprite banks; native entities remain rendered from independent assets without forced UpdateEdit. |
| Save/Load and classic | Original serializers/dirty policy/307-record supported path retained; classic regression stays unchanged. |

Keep screenshots for each component family and workspace preset, narrow/large sizes, native focus/keyboard states and 100/150/200% scale, plus actual mixed-DPI/monitor recovery where hardware permits. Screenshots establish presentation; state/action receipts establish behavior. New frontend verification must pin the agreed clock build explicitly, independently of the earlier concurrent frame-rule work.

Retained earlier bounded check: [invariance/result.json](../evidence/enhanced-mode/invariance/result.json) compares six fields on the original pinned build with/without modern view and no camera input: seed60332, MapPnt{25,0}, iteration57, plane2, spider1024/512 matched. This remains evidence for that narrow current implementation only, not a pass for the proposed workspace or the full target above.

# Phased implementation plan: remove old presentation by semantic subsystem

Every phase removes visible non-native dependencies. Preview phases are labelled incomplete; final enhanced mode has zero presented VGA screen/window/popup planes. Hidden canonical execution remains wherever it carries behavior. No phase creates a second simulation or requires editing canonical source to suit native controls.

| Phase | Concrete removal/replacement | Shippable result | Verification and evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0. Component/action contracts | Review this per-Obj/code component census and catalog. Give each action a complete entry/prelude/parameter/wait contract; distinguish L/E/T/H/U. Add read-only snapshot producers and a deterministic action executor. U entries remain blocked, not invented. | Reviewable contracts and regression fixtures; no claim of implemented native UI. | Trace component->action->source; audit transitive writes, clock reads, RNG, hidden draw effects and source static state. Reproduce both retained bugs. |
| 1. Independent native world/content snapshots | Replace canonical-bank/VGA-cache/hosted-overlay dependency with immutable native assets and semantic world/pose/message/card data. Preserve effectful source calls hidden. | World/entities/overlays correct through source modal waits; no hybrid pixel patch in that view. | Rebirth/music regression, all source planes/terrain/scent/player/spider/mower/balloons, extra paints and asset/cache pressure. Pixel/pose references plus passive state equality. |
| 2. Native workspace and passive panel lifecycle | Remove integer-scaled desktop root and original frame geometry from enhanced shell. Implement dock tree, split/tab/float/collapse, keyboard, minimums, presets/persistence, native Prompt host, one primary+read-only inspectors. Source logical lifetime remains independent. | Native workspace preview with live passive views; every layout operation is host-only. | All H-actions against stationary-layout controls; no source win_* calls, source time reads or pointer/key leakage. Inspector all-plane proof before enabling cross-plane views. Layout/DPI/screenshots and owned-window cleanup. |
| 3. Reinvented command panels and actionable world | Remove original Game/Map/Yard button rows, world scrollbars and pixel-derived hit routing. Drive catalog actions from Ant/World Controls, Colony Operations, native canvas and shared menu/keyboard registry. Retain E/T source execution invisibly where required. | Core gameplay in a modern workspace without old widget replicas. | Cross-control action equivalence, source owner leases/flush/first-click, far targets independent of layout, source navigation and yard/queen restrictions. Screenshots of each feedback/eligibility state. |
| 4. Allocation, information, graphs and tools | Remove VGA triangles, graphs, card clients, inspection/population views and all prox/editor menu textures. Replace with action-oriented allocation editors, native charts/tables/text and Tools/context controls. | All ordinary game/experiment data tools native and independently dockable. | Reachable integer allocations and release tails, preset/history/card state, ten-series remapping/four-slot eviction, links/Previous, brush repetitions and modifiers. Keyboard/UIA and passive panel visibility tests. |
| 5. Complete lifecycle, dialogs/files/help | Remove remaining intro/chooser/event/tutorial/error/About/FileSelect/save-under presentation. Use native workspace content and task/picker services at exact source boundaries. | Fully native enhanced mode with one canonical engine; classic intact. | All source wait/default/timeout/cancel, source-selected frames/text, file/dirty/error/save compatibility, active task under layout switching, menus/accelerators/text-field isolation. Static/runtime audit rejects hosted planes in native UI. |
| 6. Product hardening and deletion | Remove superseded enhanced-hosting presentation code when no remaining consumer needs it; retain compatibility execution/evidence. Finalize layouts, native accessibility, monitor recovery and performance. | Release candidate with documented limits and no hidden second game model. | Full action/passive matrix, established original/canonical/native checks where applicable, classic regressions, actual mixed-DPI/monitor/Alt+F4/minimize/restore and keyboard/UIA sessions, no leaked owned processes. Repository gates/commit only in a later write-authorized implementation task. |

Removing a source draw producer is a separate proof task from removing its visible raster output. The native workspace can be complete while original drawing still runs privately for RNG/timer/input/animation effects. The plan must never replace that execution with a no-op just because the pixels are no longer shown.

# Risks and architectural proof obligations

| Risk | Concrete source dependency | Required treatment |
|---|---|---|
| Renderer uses temporary resource residency as model | Confirmed `myBeginSong -> edit_release_life_tiles`; `world_view::life_record` | Independent immutable native asset ownership; never change source residency to repair presentation. |
| Repaint consumes RNG or changes timers | DrawSpider/DrawPalps/DrawCurBalloons and transient lists in `edit_view.c` | Preserve original calls/cadence hidden; capture source-selected results once. Repaint consumes no RNG and cannot rearm balloons. |
| Native visibility changes source execution | win_IsWinOpen, window hooks, DrawHistory/Info/control animations, viewport cache | Native docking tree and source logical lifetime are independent. Showing/hiding tabs cannot run source open/close/cleanup; action preparation is deterministic and leased where required. |
| First click lost or delivered twice | native raise-and-eat + win_Open flush + front-owner check | Durable intent queue, activation barrier and once-only source delivery; explicit modal/drag owner. |
| Native input bypasses object semantics | win_dispatch_object_event handles selection/group feedback, lists/sliders and double-click history | Use a complete audited action entry where available; otherwise retain original dispatcher/transaction effects. A bare business call that skips preparation/commit is insufficient. |
| Nested loops do not run main DoEvent | DoExpMenu, win_DoProxMenu, YellowDialog, control trackers, processExp | Per-wait semantic adapters; no reentrant DoEvent or simulation catch-up loop. Poll held state where the source does. |
| New canvas exposes more world than hidden viewport | MapPnt is saved and used by TileIsVisible, follow, clicks, source draw and overview | Explicit action-equivalence contract. Do not silently resize the engine viewport or change source visibility to native client size. |
| Canonical/world and map scent differ | ELayerMode versus MapMode; Win16 ribbon couples them differently | Separate labelled scope, same original DOS event paths; no imported Win16 mutation. |
| Triangle percentages are not simple independent sliders | fixed-point 0xFFFF fractions, GetTriLatDist/SetTriLatPoint, cvtLevels2IdealCaste | Reinvent the allocation control, but prove its source-reachable value/gesture mapping and preserve the original conversion/commit tail; no new percentage normalizer. |
| Card/help semantic information is created during drawing | DisplayCard produces link rectangles; picture-only help; transient text in PictureDialog | Observe typed card/link/text/resource metadata and call arguments. Native text layout owns hit boxes but sends the equivalent source link action. A stable link-ID adapter must be proved before dropping old draw-generated link tables. |
| Yard visual routines have behavior | DrawSimKid/DrawSwarm/balloons, animation set creation, warning deadlines | Audit state writes/RNG per producer; source chooses poses/timing, native draws snapshots. Yard is a substantial renderer, not a scaled screenshot. |
| Native file picker blocks source or changes save state | FileSelect path/current directory, SaveGame/LoadGame, pending menu/dirty prompts | Return at exact service boundary; service original waits/time/audio policy, use virtual-path mapping; serializer remains canonical. |
| Native text localization corrupts engine strings | source mutable menu state bytes, formatted object text, card links and DOS charset | Copy/decode into native catalog; never replace source strings/pointers with Unicode or translated text. Native text entry uses the OS layout/IME, bridge only values accepted by source. |
| Independent volume changes audio authority | OptionStates enable/disable source commands; native emulator mixes output | Master gain is postmix presentation; separate music/effects gain requires tagged source streams without command changes. |
| Reference drafts overclaim facts | Win16 pool stubs/private handle hypotheses; absent Mac scroll report | Keep provenance labels and unproved mappings. Resolve only the source/architecture questions needed; do not resume broad historical reconstruction. |


Additional workspace obligations: all-plane read-only views cannot use temporary global-plane swaps; panel focus cannot alter a source front-window branch; layout restore cannot replay old game state; native pointer/key traffic cannot leak into source edge scrolling; passive score/cards/history views need clean read seams rather than calling effectful UI routines. These are engineering acceptance gates, not owner-preference questions.

# Capture index and investigation limits

[Scratch image: Current primary states and hosted clients](../build/workers/enhanced-design/current-contact-sheet.png)

The linked table contains the individual captures, including the six experiment submenus. Renderer captures preserve complete window content at logical resolution and omit OS captions; `native-probe` supplies actual native chrome/menu-bar evidence with the observer-DPI limitation stated earlier. These are screenshots of the **existing** implementation, not mockups of the recommendation.

All successful capture runs used smoke exits and reported no remaining windows. The failed exploratory launches (initial missing adjacent font resources and the first Full-tools recipe) were terminated/cleaned up. Debugger runs completed without inferior memory writes or game-function calls. The [final process/output audit](../build/workers/enhanced-design/final-audit.json) found no remaining investigation-owned game processes, no windows left by successful capture runs, and no broken local document links. No process belonging to the concurrent worker was stopped.

The component/action design is a source-backed specification, not an implemented or whole-state-verified frontend. The following historical evidence remains incomplete: all rare-window screenshots, an on-hardware trace of the owner's exact multi-click count, full Mac control/camera-code correspondence, the missing Mac scroll report, and the Win16 last yard-bookmark dispatch discrepancy. None blocks the confirmed sprite-lifetime root cause or the required native architecture. They are not reasons to retain visible VGA clients or invent mappings. The expanded component tables explicitly retain unresolved semantics; no U action is implementation-ready. Win16 runtime observation is bounded as documented above.

Microsoft primary guidance consulted for the native proposal: [Common Item Dialog](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/shell/common-file-dialog) for modern open/save integration; [High DPI desktop development](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/hidpi/high-dpi-desktop-application-development-on-windows) for dynamic monitor/DPI changes; [Keyboard accessibility](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/apps/design/accessibility/keyboard-accessibility) for intentional focus traversal and operable controls. These support the platform design, not claims about historical SimAnt behavior.
